CREATE TABLE customers (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
  name TEXT NOT NULL,
  city TEXT NOT NULL
);
CREATE TABLE orders (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
  customer_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES customers(id),
  total REAL NOT NULL,
  status TEXT NOT NULL
);
